At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

</details>


### [151] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2 是一个基于 xLSTM 的循环时间序列基础模型，支持单变量和多变量预测，能够处理过去和未来的协变量，在流式场景下保持恒定推理成本，并在零样本任务上达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的时间序列基础模型在捕获跨变量依赖时具有二次复杂度和需要全历史重计算的问题，无法高效处理流式到达的观测数据。

Method: 采用基于 xLSTM 的循环架构，结合双向时间混合器与非对称分组注意力变量混合器，支持未来已知协变量的因果集成；提出合成耦合管道从单变量语料库动态生成多变量样本以支持可扩展预训练。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 上达到零样本 SOTA；在流式任意上下文长度下保持稳定；恒定每补丁推理成本；单变量模式 38.4M 参数，多变量模式额外 44.1M 参数。

Conclusion: TiRex-2 是首个同时具备流式高效推理、未来协变量集成和严格因果性的时间序列基础模型。

Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [A Priori Integral Persistent Excitation in Conservative Polynomial ODEs with Higher-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00091)
*Aleksandr Semenov,Alexander Fradkov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在保守多项式常微分方程中验证积分持续激励条件的方法，无需先验参数知识，并在Lean定理证明器中完成了主要结果的严格形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性动力系统的参数辨识与自适应控制中，积分持续激励条件至关重要，但现有方法通常需要参数先验知识或缺乏严格验证手段。

Method: 针对保守多项式常微分方程，提出一种无需参数先验知识即可验证积分持续激励的方法，并利用Lean形式化验证语言对主要定理进行了机器可验证的严格证明。

Result: 所提方法能有效验证积分持续激励条件，给出了相应定理的严格证明，并通过一个具有高阶相互作用的非线性动力系统实例分析了方法的应用效果。

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性系统的持续激励验证提供了形式化保证，提高了数学证明的可靠性，在参数辨识与自适应控制领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: The paper proposes an approach for verifying integral persistent excitation, which is important in problems of parameter identification and adaptive control in nonlinear dynamical systems. The approach works for conservative polynomial ODEs a priori without knowledge of the parameters. Rigorous proofs of the corresponding theorems are provided. An example of a nonlinear dynamical system with higher-order interactions and the application of the proposed method to it are analyzed. The proof of the main result is formalized in the Lean formal verification language.

</details>


### [153] [Homogenization of $\ell_2$-Adversarial Training in High-Dimensions: Exact Dynamics under Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00207)
*Fabrizzio Sabelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对高维高斯混合模型上的$\ell_2$对抗训练，在流式SGD下建立了学习动力学的分析框架。推导了SGD迭代统计量的确定性等价，研究了Polyak步长和精确线搜索两种学习率调度。对于$\ell_2$对抗最小二乘，发现不同于无噪声标准最小二乘，无常数学习率能保证单调下降；各向异性协方差和岭参数不匹配是精确线搜索次优性的主要原因。引入对抗同质化SDE，证明其风险演化等价于具有自适应学习率和自适应$\ell_2$正则化的标准最小二乘SGD。收敛时，极限对抗风险和SGD迭代由不动点方程确定，等价于岭回归问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维对抗训练中SGD的学习动力学，尤其是学习率调度的影响，以及对抗风险与SGD迭代的收敛行为。

Method: 理论推导：针对单指标模型和高斯混合，建立SGD迭代统计量的确定性等价（ODE系统）；引入对抗同质化SDE；分析Polyak步长和精确线搜索两种学习率调度。

Result: 1) 对抗最小二乘中无常数学习率保证单调下降；2) 各向异性协方差和岭参数不匹配导致精确线搜索次优；3) 对抗风险演化等价于带自适应学习率和自适应$\ell_2$正则化的标准最小二乘SGD；4) 极限收敛解等价于岭回归问题。

Conclusion: 为高维对抗训练的SGD动力学提供了完整理论框架，揭示了学习率调度和正则化效应的关键作用，建立了对抗训练与标准岭回归之间的等价关系。

Abstract: We develop a framework for analyzing the learning dynamics of $\ell_2$-adversarial training of single-index models on Gaussian mixtures in the high-dimensional limit under streaming stochastic gradient descent (SGD). We derive deterministic equivalents for a broad class of statistics of the SGD iterates, including the adversarial risk and distance to adversarial optimality, in terms of the solution to a system of ODEs. We use them to study two idealized learning rate schedules: the Polyak stepsize and exact line search. In the case of $\ell_2$-adversarial least squares with a single class, we show that, unlike noiseless standard least squares, no constant learning rate guarantees monotone descent of SGD towards a minimizer of the adversarial risk. We identify anisotropic covariance and a mismatch in ridge parameters as the main sources of suboptimality of exact line search relative to the Polyak stepsize. We also introduce a stochastic differential equation (SDE), called adversarial homogenized SGD, that captures the evolution of statistics of the iterates of SGD. For $\ell_2$-adversarial least squares, using this SDE, we show the evolution of the risk is equivalent, up to dimension-free constants, to that of SGD on standard least squares with an adaptive learning rate and adaptive $\ell_2$-regularization. When the dynamics converge, the limiting adversarial risk and SGD iterate are determined by a fixed-point equation, with the limiting iterate being equivalent to the solution of a ridge regression problem whose regularization parameter is the limiting effective regularization of SGD.

</details>


### [154] [Killing the Case for Randomization in Dynamic Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00328)
*Mikhail Fadin,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去随机化算法，能够将任何基于采样的库存无关策略转化为确定性策略序列，同时不降低性能。进一步地，论文研究了局部最优确定性策略，并证明其性能保证为1/2 - epsilon，与最优采样策略相比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态分类优化问题中，基于采样的库存无关策略虽然广泛使用，但采样过程引入了额外的性能不确定性。现有研究已知库存无关性不会影响性能，但采样带来的随机性尚未被有效消除。

Method: 提出去随机化算法，将采样策略转化为确定性策略序列；进一步提出变体算法，在原始策略支持范围外搜索确定性序列；利用静态分类优化问题的可解性保证算法效率；分析局部最优确定性策略的理论性能保证。

Result: 去随机化后的确定性策略不降低原策略性能；局部最优确定性策略在较最优采样策略时具有1/2 - epsilon的性能保证。

Conclusion: 采样带来的随机性可以通过去随机化算法消除，且局部最优确定性策略具有常数因子性能保证，为动态分类优化提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: One of the traditional approaches for constructing approximate policies for dynamic assortment optimization problems is to use sampling-based inventory-agnostic policies. Such policies are called sampling-based, as they sample an assortment of products from a fixed distribution at each time period to offer to a customer of each type. Such policies are called inventory-agnostic, as the sampled assortments may include products without remaining inventories, so if a customer chooses a product without remaining inventories, then she leaves without a purchase. Inventory-agnostic nature of a policy is not a concern, because it is known that if the policy samples an assortment that includes products without remaining inventories, then dropping the products without remaining inventories does not degrade the performance. However, sampling-based nature of a policy is a concern, because sampling brings another source of uncertainty in the performance. In this paper, we give an algorithm to de-randomize any sampling-based inventory-agnostic policy, so the de-randomized policy offers a deterministic sequence of assortments within the support of the original policy without degrading the performance. Furthermore, we give a variation of our de-randomization algorithm that searches for a deterministic sequence of assortments beyond the support of the original policy. We show that we can implement the latter variation efficiently as long as we can solve the static assortment optimization problem under the choice model governing the choice process of the customers. As our crowning technical contribution, we study locally-optimal deterministic policies, where changing any single one of the assortments in the policy does not improve the total expected revenue. We show that any locally-optimal policy has a performance guarantee of 1/2 - epsilon when compared with the best sampling-based policy.

</details>


### [155] [Regularized Model Predictive Control via Contractivity and Implicit Lur'e Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00383)
*Ryotaro Shima,Anand Gokhale,Alexander Davydov,Francesco Bullo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对正则化模型预测控制（MPC）提出了一种基于收缩理论的稳定性分析方法，通过将正则化MPC解释为隐式Lur'e系统，推导了线性矩阵不等式条件，并在三类广泛的正则化器上验证了闭环性能与收缩稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化MPC的反馈律由带额外正则化成本的有限时域最优控制问题隐式定义，其稳定性分析缺乏统一的收缩理论框架。

Method: 将正则化MPC解释为隐式Lur'e系统（正则化成本扰动最优性条件），提出针对隐式Lur'e系统的乘子基收缩框架，推导适用于三类正则化器的线性矩阵不等式条件。

Result: 在输入/状态软惩罚、硬输入约束和稀疏性促进惩罚上的数值实验表明，正则化在保持形式化收缩稳定性保证的同时塑造了闭环性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的乘子基收缩框架为带有多种正则化器的MPC提供了统一的稳定性分析工具，且正则化有助于在保证稳定性的前提下优化控制性能。

Abstract: This paper develops a contraction-based stability analysis for regularized model predictive control (MPC), whose feedback law is defined implicitly by a finite-horizon optimal control problem with an additional regularizing cost. The proposed approach interprets regularized MPC as an implicit Lur'e system, in which the regularizing cost perturbs the optimality conditions. We develop a multiplier-based contraction framework for implicit Lur'e systems and derive linear matrix inequality conditions for regularized MPC with three broad classes of regularizers: convex smooth stage costs, convex closed proper stage costs, and differentiable regularizers with Lipschitz gradients. Numerical studies on input and state soft penalties, hard input constraints, and sparsity-promoting penalties illustrate that regularization shapes closed-loop performance while retaining formal contraction-based stability guarantees.

</details>


### [156] [L2-L2-gain bounds for quadratic output systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00552)
*Birgit Hillebrecht*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了线性时不变系统在输出为状态和输入的二次函数时的L2-L2增益显式上界，该上界在纯二次输出情况下等于双变量传递函数沿频率域反对角线的L2范数，并通过求解线性矩阵方程实现计算，为二次输出模型的评估与降阶提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端口哈密顿系统、最优控制和随机问题等领域中，系统输出常为状态和输入的二次函数，但缺乏对此类系统L2增益的显式上界估计和高效计算方法。

Method: 通过对二次输出系统进行线性矩阵方程求解，推导了L2-L2增益的显式上界；当输出仅为状态的纯二次函数时，该上界等于双变量传递函数在频率域反对角线{(s,-s)|s∈iℝ}上的L2范数。

Result: 得到了二次输出线性时不变系统L2-L2增益的显式上界，并建立了该上界与双变量传递函数在反对角线上的L2范数之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 该结果为二次输出模型的评估与模型降阶提供了实用的计算工具。

Abstract: We derive an explicit bound for the L2-L2-gain of linear time-invariant systems whose output is a quadratic function of the state and the input. Such systems appear naturally in many areas, for example for port-Hamiltonian systems, optimal-control, and stochastic problems. In case the output is purely quadratic in the state, the bound equals the L2-norm of the bivariate transfer function evaluated along the anti-diagonal $\{(s,\,-s)\mid s\in i\mathbb R\}$ of the $i \mathbb R\times i \mathbb R$ frequency domain. Further, we show how the bound can be computed by solving linear matrix equations. This result provides a practical tool for assessing and reducing quadratic-output models.

</details>


### [157] [Vehicle Routing Problem Meets Large Language Models: An Overview and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00604)
*Xianchao Xiu,Chong Shen,Yanjiao Zhu,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用大语言模型（LLM）解决车辆路径问题（VRP）及其变体的研究进展，将LLM在路由优化中的角色归纳为三种：建模者（将自然语言需求转化为约束和代码）、设计者（生成启发式策略和路径方案）、协调者（组织工具调用、多智能体协作和神经求解器连接），并梳理了相关基准、数据集和评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRP及其变体是NP-hard问题，实际路由任务常包含时间窗、容量、取送货、动态请求等多种约束，建模和求解困难。LLM提供了灵活的自然语言接口，能够处理需求、生成代码、推理约束和调用外部工具，为路由优化开辟了新途径。本文旨在系统梳理这一新兴方向的研究进展。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统整理LLM驱动的VRP研究，将现有工作按三种角色（建模者、设计者、协调者）分类组织，同时回顾标准基准、真实/近真实运营数据集、面向LLM的评估框架，并开展了两个对比实验。

Result: 梳理了LLM在VRP中的三种应用角色：建模者（自然语言转约束和代码）、设计者（生成启发式/算子/路径方案）、协调者（组织工具调用、多智能体协作、连接神经求解器）；整理了相关基准、数据集和评估框架，并通过对比实验给出了初步发现。

Conclusion: LLM为路由优化提供了灵活的接口和能力，当前研究已覆盖建模、设计和协调三种角色，但在实际部署中仍需进一步验证和标准化评估。本文为智能决策、先进制造和工业自动化中的LLM辅助路由优化提供了结构化参考。

Abstract: The vehicle routing problem (VRP) is a central optimization problem in artificial intelligence, logistics automation, transportation scheduling, and industrial decision-making. VRP and its variants are NP-hard, and practical routing tasks often combine time windows, vehicle capacities, pickup-and-delivery relations, dynamic requests, and other operational constraints, making both modeling and solving difficult. Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for routing optimization by processing natural-language requirements, generating code, reasoning over constraints, and interacting with external tools. This survey reviews LLM-driven research on VRP, covering the basic definition, main variants, major solver families, and LLM concepts needed for this topic. Existing studies are organized into three roles: modelers translate natural-language requirements into constraints and modeling code; designers generate heuristics, operators, or route plans; and coordinators organize tool calls, multi-agent collaboration, and connections with neural solvers. The survey also reviews standard benchmarks, real or near-real operational datasets, LLM-oriented evaluation frameworks, and two comparative experiments. The goal is to clarify current progress in LLM-assisted routing optimization and provide a structured reference for intelligent decision-making, advanced manufacturing, and industrial automation.

</details>


### [158] [An semidefinite programming-based $\varepsilon$-constraint method for the bi-objective single-row facility layout problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00611)
*Christof Brandstetter,Elisabeth Gaar,Markus Sinnl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多目标单行设施布局问题（MO-SRFLP），并基于ε-约束法和半定规划（SDP）分支定界程序开发了求解算法，通过非二元分支和节点重用等增强策略，在双目标情形下展示了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的SRFLP拓展到多目标场景（如多个加权和目标），并在Pareto最优性框架下求解，填补现有研究在该方向上的空白。

Method: 基于ε-约束法求解双目标SRFLP；采用基于半定规划（SDP）的分支定界过程替代传统黑箱ILP求解器；提出非二元分支和分支定界树节点重用等增强策略。

Result: 通过计算实验证明了所提求解方法及其增强策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于SDP的分支定界方法配合ε-约束法能有效求解多目标SRFLP，且非二元分支和节点重用等增强策略能够进一步提升算法性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a multi-objective version of the well-known single-row facility layout problem (SRFLP). In the SRFLP, a set of one-dimensional facilities should be placed along a single line such that the weighted sum of the center-to-center distances of each pair of facilities is minimized. In our multi-objective extension, there are multiple such weighted-sum objectives which we consider under the concept of Pareto optimality.
  We develop a solution algorithm based on the $\varepsilon$-constraint method to solve the bi-objective SRFLP. Many existing works on the $\varepsilon$-constraint method use integer linear programming (ILP) solvers in a black-box fashion for solving the problems at the individual iterations of the method. In contrast to that, we use our own branch-and-bound procedure based on semidefinite programming (SDP), as SDP relaxations are known to be more effective for solving the SRFLP than linear programming relaxations of ILPs. This allows us to propose several enhancements procedures for our $\varepsilon$-constraint approach, such as non-binary branching and reusing of nodes within the branch-and-bound trees, which are usually not possible when using black-box solvers. We present a computational study to demonstrate the effectiveness of our solution approach and its enhancements.

</details>


### [159] [Iterative graph lifting for automatic design of path-complete stability certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00637)
*Léa Ninite,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图论分析的迭代算法，通过优化路径完整图来逼近切换线性系统的联合谱半径（JSR），并在数值实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切换线性系统在任意切换下的稳定性是控制理论中的基本问题，与联合谱半径（JSR）密切相关。路径完整方法为JSR近似提供了框架，但如何高效且精简地构造路径完整图以精确逼近JSR仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出一种迭代算法：首先推导路径完整图达到精确JSR的充分条件；若不满足条件，通过分析主动约束诱导的图结构识别瓶颈节点，进而利用局部图提升（节点分裂）来细化路径完整图，重复此过程直至收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明，该算法在效率和可扩展性方面优于现有最先进方法，在所有具有挑战性的测试实例上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文的图论分析与迭代细化策略为JSR近似提供了一种高效、精简的求解途径，能够通过局部图操作逐步提升精度，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Stability of switched linear systems under arbitrary switching is a fundamental problem in control theory, closely related to the joint spectral radius (JSR), which characterizes the worst-case growth rate of system trajectories. In this paper, we contribute to the path-complete approach for approximating the JSR. This framework constructs algebraic stability certificates using labeled directed graphs, known as path-complete graphs. These certificates can be computed via an associated optimization problem. We propose an iterative algorithm that refines path-complete graphs in an efficient and parsimonious manner. The algorithm relies on a graph-theoretic analysis of the optimality conditions of the underlying optimization problem. In particular, we derive a sufficient condition under which the exact JSR is attained by a given path-complete graph. When this condition is not satisfied, we identify bottleneck nodes by analyzing the graph induced by the active constraints. We then use this information to refine the path-complete graph via local graph lifting (node splitting), and repeat the procedure. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed approach, outperforming state-of-the-art methods on all challenging instances tested.

</details>


### [160] [Effective dynamics of the Sinkhorn algorithm in the regime of low entropy regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00665)
*Guillaume Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sinkhorn算法在小正则化参数τ下的冷启动现象，证明了当τ→0时，Sinkhorn迭代在时间缩放t=τk下收敛到一条连续时间曲线，表征了"冷Sinkhorn"动力学，并证明该动力学在有限时间内收敛到无正则化解，从而给出了Sinkhorn算法的新保证：在k=O(τ^{-1})次迭代内达到Õ(τ)的对偶次优性，优于传统分析的O(τ^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sinkhorn算法分析给出的迭代复杂度为k=O(τ^{-2})，但实际观测中小正则化参数下算法行为表现出独特的分段线性与鞍点到鞍点特征，作者希望从理论上解释这一现象并得到更紧的收敛保证。

Method: 通过分析当正则化参数τ→0时Sinkhorn迭代的渐近行为，将离散迭代重新缩放为连续时间曲线t=τk，推导出冷Sinkhorn动力学的显式表达式，并证明该动力学具有类似单纯形算法的对偶优化性质。

Result: 证明了冷Sinkhorn动力学在有限时间内收敛到无正则化最优传输问题的解，进而推导出Sinkhorn算法在k=O(τ^{-1})次迭代内达到Õ(τ)对偶次优性，将传统分析的O(τ^{-2})复杂度降低了阶数。

Conclusion: 该工作为Sinkhorn算法在小正则化参数下的加速收敛提供了理论解释，建立了与单纯形算法的类比，并为实际使用中小τ值下Sinkhorn的高效性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Sinkhorn algorithm is the de facto standard method for numerically solving entropy-regularized optimal transport problems over finite sets. In this work, we investigate a phenomenon arising when Sinkhorn is applied with a small regularization parameter $τ$: the evolution of the dual variables (the logarithm of the scaling factors) is approximately piecewise-linear, while the primal variables (the approximate transport plans) exhibit a saddle-to-saddle type behavior. We prove that as $τ\to 0$, the Sinkhorn iterates indeed converge to a continuous-time curve consistent with these observations, when time is rescaled as $t = τk$, and we characterize the limiting "cold Sinkhorn" dynamics explicitly. In particular, we show that it acts as a dual optimization dynamics for the unregularized problem with properties analogous to the simplex algorithm. Notably, this dynamics converges in finite time to an unregularized solution, implying a novel guarantee for the Sinkhorn algorithm itself: it achieves $\tilde{O}(τ)$ dual suboptimality in $k = O(τ^{-1})$ iterations, instead of $k = O(τ^{-2})$ as existing analyses would suggest.

</details>


### [161] [Geometric Reduced-Attitude Tracking Under a Time-Varying Conic Constraint via Smooth Reference-Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00793)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了刚体在二维球面上的降阶姿态跟踪问题，提出了一种在时变锥约束下的几何跟踪律与光滑参考整形机制，实现了几乎全局渐近和区域指数收敛，并通过软约束方式满足了时变锥约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有刚体姿态跟踪方法在处理时变锥约束（如传感器视场限制、避障等）时缺乏有效的理论保证，需要一种兼具强稳定性保证与光滑控制输入的方法。

Method: 1）基于S2运动学模型，设计了几何跟踪律实现无约束情况下的收敛；2）引入光滑参考整形机制，通过调整期望方向来满足时变锥约束，同时保留跟踪律所需的光滑性；3）采用软约束方式替代硬约束。

Result: 所提方法实现了几乎全局渐近稳定和区域指数收敛，能有效满足时变锥约束（软约束版本），仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于需要确定性行为、光滑控制作用和强稳定性保证的应用场景，但代价是约束只能被软性满足而非严格满足。

Abstract: This letter studies reduced-attitude tracking for a rigid body on the 2-sphere S2 under a time-varying conic constraint. Using a kinematic model on S2, we first propose a geometric tracking law that guarantees almostglobal asymptotic and regionally exponential convergence in the unconstrained case, where the angular velocity serves as the control input. We then introduce a smooth reference-shaping mechanism that adjusts the desired direction so that the reference provided to the controller satisfies the time-varying conic constraint while preserving the smoothness required by the tracking law. The resulting approach yields smooth continuous feedback and retains the stability guarantees of the unconstrained controller, albeit at the expense of enforcing a soft version of the original constraint. Simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and highlight its suitability for applications where deterministic behavior, smooth control action, and strong stability guarantees are preferred over hard constraint satisfaction.

</details>


### [162] [Optimal control problem for reflected McKean--Vlasov stochastic differential equations with Poisson jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00799)
*Wenrui Lu,Kai Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带Poisson跳的反射McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的最优松弛控制问题，在Lipschitz和适当增长条件下建立了状态过程与反射过程的均匀矩估计，利用Aldous紧性准则、Skorokhod映射连续性和随机积分稳定性证明了最优松弛控制的存在性，并在Roxin凸性条件下证明了严格最优控制的存在性，同时展示了松弛控制可由严格控制序列逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的最优控制研究多集中于连续扩散情形，缺乏对带跳反射过程在Skorokhod空间中的分析框架，且跳项的存在使得状态过程属于Skorokhod空间$D([0,T],\Rp)$，紧性证明和极限传递更为复杂，需要建立新的理论工具。

Method: 首先在Lipschitz条件和适当增长条件下建立状态过程与反射过程的均匀矩估计；其次利用Aldous紧性准则、Skorokhod映射的连续性以及随机积分的稳定性结果证明最优松弛控制的存在性；然后在Roxin凸性条件下证明严格最优控制的存在性；最后在一般情形下展示松弛控制可由严格控制序列逼近。

Result: 成功证明了带Poisson跳的一维反射McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的最优松弛控制存在性；在Roxin凸性条件下，进一步证明了严格最优控制的存在性；在一般情形下，松弛控制可由严格控制序列逼近。

Conclusion: 本文为带跳反射McKean-Vlasov系统的控制问题提供了完整的松弛控制框架，解决了Skorokhod空间中紧性传递的难点，并建立了松弛控制与严格控制之间的桥梁关系。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the optimal relaxed control problem for a class of one-dimensional reflected McKean--Vlasov stochastic differential equations with Poisson jumps. Due to the presence of the jump term, the state process generally belongs to the Skorokhod space $D([0,T],\Rp)$, which makes the proof of tightness and the passage to the limit more complicated. Under Lipschitz conditions and suitable growth conditions, we establish uniform moment estimates for the state process and the reflecting process. Then, by using Aldous' tightness criterion, the continuity of the Skorokhod map, and the stability results for stochastic integrals, we prove the existence of an optimal relaxed control. Furthermore, under the Roxin convexity condition, we prove the existence of a strict optimal control. In the general case, we show that relaxed controls can be approximated by a sequence of strict controls.

</details>


### [163] [Experiment Design for Set-membership Identification: From Prior Knowledge to Universal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00844)
*Amir Shakouri,Henk J. van Waarde,M. Kanat Camlibel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了未知线性时不变系统的输入信号设计问题，提出了"通用输入"概念及其设计方法，能在有限时域内生成适合精确辨识的数据。该方法推广了基于Willems基本引理的持续激励输入方法，适用于更广泛的先验知识场景，且在噪声环境下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于持续激励输入的实验设计方法受限于系统可控性这一先验知识，无法适用于其他类型的先验知识。本文旨在设计一种通用输入方法，使其能够适用于更广泛的先验知识场景，并在样本效率和噪声环境下的精确辨识方面取得更好效果。

Method: 论文提出了"通用输入"（universal inputs）的概念，即应用于任何符合先验知识的系统时，都能产生适合准确辨识的数据。作者分别考虑了无噪声和有噪声（$\ell_\infty$有界噪声）两种场景，并纳入了系统参数的一般先验知识，在此基础上设计了新的通用输入生成方法。

Result: 所提出的通用输入方法推广了基于Willems基本引理且依赖于持续激励输入的实验设计方法；对于其他类型的先验知识，存在样本效率更高的通用输入；在某些先验知识下，可以在噪声环境中实现精确辨识的实验设计。

Conclusion: 通用输入方法显著拓展了系统辨识实验设计的适用范围，不仅突破了持续激励输入对系统可控性的依赖，还在样本效率和噪声鲁棒性方面展现出优势，为具有不同先验知识的系统辨识提供了更灵活有效的手段。

Abstract: We consider the problem of designing input signals for an unknown linear time-invariant system in such a way that the resulting data, within a finite horizon, is suitable for identification with a desired accuracy. We consider both noise-free and noisy settings with $\ell_\infty$--bounded noise models. We will take into account general prior knowledge of the system parameters. Central in our study is the concept of universal inputs. An input is called universal for identification if, when applied to any system complying with the prior knowledge, it yields data suitable for accurate identification. We provide new methods for designing such universal inputs. Our results generalize the experiment design approach based on Willems et al.'s fundamental lemma that relies on persistently exciting inputs, and that is limited to prior knowledge on controllability. It turns out that for other types of prior knowledge, there exist universal inputs that outperform the persistently exciting ones, e.g., in terms of sample efficiency. Moreover, we investigate types of prior knowledge that enable experiment design for exact identification in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [164] [On directional local minimality and directional optimality conditions in nonsmooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00882)
*Timm Baake,Patrick Mehlitz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对下半连续函数的无约束最小化问题，利用一阶/二阶次导数、方向极限次微分和方向近端次微分，基于方向局部极小性概念，推导了必要充分的一阶和二阶最优性条件，并给出了经典非方向局部极小性的最优性条件，通过临界方向相关变分对象进行表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典非方向最优性条件在分析某些非光滑优化问题时分辨率不足，需要引入方向性概念以进行更精细的刻画和判定。

Method: 利用方向极限次微分、方向近端次微分以及一阶/二阶次导数等方向性变分分析工具，基于方向局部极小性定义，推导必要充分的最优性条件，并进一步将其转化为涉及临界方向的经典非方向最优性条件。

Result: 得到了方向局部极小性的必要充分一阶和二阶最优性条件，以及经典非方向局部极小性在临界方向框架下的最优性条件。

Conclusion: 推导的条件比经典非方向最优性条件具有更高的分辨率，能够实现更精细的最优性分析。

Abstract: This paper considers the unconstrained minimization of a lower semicontinuous function. Exploiting first and second subderivatives, directional limiting subdifferentials, and directional proximal subdifferentials, necessary and sufficient first- and second-order optimality conditions are derived that build upon the recently introduced notion of directional local minimality. These results then also yield optimality conditions for conventional nondirectional local minimality which are stated in terms of so-called critical directions and variational objects depending on them. Illustrative examples show that the derived conditions allow for a finer analysis than classical nondirectional optimality conditions.

</details>


### [165] [Influence of Routing and Speed Limits on Optimal Solutions in Traffic Emission Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00944)
*Marc-André Bach,Simone Göttlich,Alena Ulke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究交通排放模型中路由策略与限速策略对最优解的影响，构建基于一阶宏观交通模型与对流-扩散模型的单目标和多目标优化问题。数值实验表明，限速策略在减排和提升交通效率方面均优于路由策略，多目标优化揭示了两种目标间的权衡，且包含限速的控制集产生的帕累托最优解严格优于仅路由控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多单独考虑路由策略或限速策略对交通排放的影响，缺乏对不同控制场景的系统比较，尤其缺少对两种策略联合优化与单独优化效果的量化对比。

Method: 基于一阶宏观交通模型（LWR模型）与对流-扩散排放模型，构建单目标和多目标优化问题；对比三种控制场景：仅优化路由、仅优化限速、同时优化两者；在小型路网上进行数值实验。

Result: 限速策略在减少排放和提升交通效率方面始终优于路由策略；多目标优化显示两个目标之间存在权衡；包含限速的控制集产生的帕累托最优解严格优于仅路由控制的解。

Conclusion: 限速策略是比路由策略更有效的交通排放控制手段，联合优化可提供更优的帕累托前沿，为兼顾交通效率与环保目标的交通管理提供定量指导。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of routing strategies and speed limit policies on optimal solutions in traffic emission models. Building on a first-order macroscopic traffic model coupled with an advection-diffusion model, we formulate single- and multi-objective optimization problems to simultaneously maximize traffic efficiency and minimize air pollution. We compare three control scenarios: optimizing only the routing strategy, optimizing only the speed limit policy, and optimizing both simultaneously. Numerical experiments on a small road network demonstrate that speed limit policies consistently achieve larger reductions in emissions and greater gains in traffic efficiency than routing strategies. Multi-objective optimization reveals the trade-off between the two goals and confirms that including speed limits in the control set yields Pareto-optimal solutions that are strictly superior to those obtained by routing control only. Our results provide quantitative guidance for traffic management seeking to balance mobility and environmental objectives.

</details>


### [166] [Computationally Efficient Near-Optimal Control for Current Ripple Reduction and Optimization of Three-Phase Motors via LMIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01215)
*Huu-Thinh Do,Trung B. Tran,Jing Sun,Ilya Kolmanovsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的永磁同步电机（PMSM）最优控制方法，通过二次参数化和迭代Bellman不等式逼近无限时域值函数，在保持与有限控制集MPC相当性能的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三相永磁同步电机的最优控制面临非线性和控制集离散性的双重挑战，现有方法（混合整数轨迹优化、值迭代过程）计算复杂度过高，亟需一种高效且性能可比的替代方案。

Method: 采用二次参数化形式逼近无限时域值函数，通过迭代Bellman不等式构建线性矩阵不等式（LMI）约束，形成可解的凸优化问题。该值函数离线高效计算，在线作为水平线-1最优控制律的尾成本使用。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在开关损耗和电流纹波之间实现了良好的权衡，性能与有限控制集MPC相当，但计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 基于LMI的值函数逼近方法为PMSM控制提供了一种离线可解、在线高效的凸优化方案，在计算效率和性能之间取得了有利平衡。

Abstract: The optimal control of three-phase permanent-magnet synchronous motors (PMSMs) is challenging due to their nonlinearity and the discrete nature of the control set. Existing approaches either rely on mixed-integer trajectory optimization or require computationally intensive value-iteration procedures. This paper proposes a Linear Matrix Inequality (LMI)-based method for approximating the infinite-horizon value function using a quadratic parameterization and iterated Bellman inequalities, yielding a tractable convex program. The computed function can be obtained efficiently offline and used online as a tail cost in a horizon-one optimal control law. Simulation results show that the proposed approach achieves a favorable trade-off between switching effort and current ripple, with performance comparable to that of finite-control-set MPC but with a significantly lower computational cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"建构性对齐"范式，将AI对齐重新定义为对人类偏好轨迹的控制问题，而非静态偏好满足。偏好是多层、动态、在与AI交互中建构的状态变量，对齐应关注AI系统如何影响人类价值形成过程，确保价值轨迹保持连贯、反思性认可、认知基础、抗操纵和赋能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法将人类偏好视为固定目标进行推断和优化，这与大量实证证据矛盾——偏好具有层次性、动态性，并通过与适应性技术的交互而建构。随着AI系统日益持久、个性化和社交嵌入，它们越来越多地参与塑造人类的注意力、价值观和认同。

Method: 借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为与AI系统交互下演化的分层状态变量；采用控制论框架，其中系统动作和交互设计共同影响世界状态和人类评价状态。

Result: 提出对齐的核心不是控制AI行为，而是调节AI系统如何影响人类偏好的演化——确保价值轨迹保持连贯、反思性认可、认知基础、抗操纵和赋能性。

Conclusion: 对齐问题转变为治理长期价值形成的问题，而非简单地满足静态偏好。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [168] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界道德"框架，将有限理性概念扩展至道德认知，论证有限智能体在道德计算中面临广度与深度的内在权衡，并将不同伦理理论解释为适应不同需求条件的局部最优策略，而非竞争性的道德真理描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将道德推理视为固定的伦理理论（道义论、后果主义、美德伦理）的静态规则或价值函数执行，忽视了真实智能体（人类或AI）在有限计算资源下面临的根本性约束。作者旨在为有限智能体的道德问题提供形式化的计算需求分析框架。

Method: 借鉴Herbert Simon的有界理性概念，提出两个正交维度：道德广度（被视为道德相关的实体范围）和道德深度（评估实体间相互作用所需的推理整合程度）。通过论证有限资源下这两个维度之间存在不可避免的权衡，定义道德计算的可达空间。

Result: 在该框架下，不同伦理理论被解释为适应不同需求模式的局部高效策略，而非相互竞争的道德真理学说。框架产生了形式化的道德遗憾和约束下道德进步的概念，并指出人工系统中的道德对齐取决于道德推理能力的规模与分配，而非对人类判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 有界道德框架为理解有限智能体的道德推理提供了形式化基础，揭示了道德计算中资源约束的根本性影响，并为AI对齐问题提供了新的视角：关键在于推理能力的扩展与配置，而非行为层面的模仿。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [169] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMM数据模型，一种结合规范性约束与自由文本标签的知识文档模型，旨在解决传统文档系统在知识结构化、更新、共享和复用方面的局限性，并通过跨学科互操作性和参考实现验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档系统虽便于大规模传播，但限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享和复用；正式方法因优先考虑形式结构而牺牲了人机可用性和普及性；AI系统虽重塑文档生产，但未提供可替代传统文档的统一便携方案。

Method: MMM模型：将少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由相结合，设计用于跨学科、跨应用、跨部署的互操作性，无需语义收敛。包含参考实现和试点部署数据。

Result: 参考实现和试点部署数据证明了MMM模型的可实现性和早期可用性。

Conclusion: MMM为跨学科协作研究中的知识文档化提供了一种平衡规范性与灵活性的数据模型，在无需语义收敛的前提下实现了互操作性，有望成为传统文档的便携替代方案。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [170] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种约束性、可验证的智能体框架，将LLM输出从自由文本转为类型化JSON采集器配置，结合六类型采集器分类法、模板/工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、规则质检和结构化反馈修正，在138个任务上验证了框架的稳定性和零推理成本的可重复执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和智能体虽能从自然语言需求生成网络爬虫，但直接生成因依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配和页面结构异质性而不可靠，亟需一种可靠、低成本且可验证的开放式网页数据采集方案。

Method: 提出约束性、可验证的智能体框架：(1)六类型采集器分类法（支持需求分类）；(2)模板和工具函数约束；(3)静态Airflow DAG执行；(4)基于规则的质检；(5)结构化反馈修正机制。将LLM输出从自由代码转为类型化JSON配置。

Result: 在138个任务上验证分类法支持基于描述的需求分类，但稳定实例化需补全来源、字段和执行约束；在80个独立验证任务中，框架实现零执行阶段LLM token消耗和最低平均挂钟时间，以适度降低的one-shot质量为代价换取可复用、确定且可验证的执行路径。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可复用、低成本、可验证的执行路径，适用于重复性的开放式网页数据采集任务。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [171] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对空中交通管制中路径规划算法与管制员实际需求脱节的问题，开发了一种基于解空间显示（solution-space displays）的冲突规避路径规划算法。该算法融合三种意图冲突检测方法（距离、时间间隔、区域），并设计顶点和边两种搜索节点变体（SSPPV和SSPPE）。实验表明，SSPPV配合区域冲突检测性能最优，在MUAC Delta扇区场景中平均计算时间仅3.69毫秒（5海里网格）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法在战术管制中采纳率低，因为算法设计优先级与管制员实际需求不匹配。亟需开发可解释、计算高效且专为人类使用的决策支持方案。

Method: 算法以解空间显示的可解释性和灵活性为设计原则，融合管制员决策逻辑（间隔标准、机动限制、航路点最小化、路由实用性）。集成三种冲突检测方法（距离、时间间隔、区域），提出顶点搜索（SSPPV）和边搜索（SSPPE）两种解空间路径规划变体。

Result: SSPPV配合区域冲突检测性能最优，在MUAC Delta扇区（5海里网格）平均计算时间3.69毫秒。

Conclusion: 基于解空间显示的SSPPV算法在计算效率和路径质量上表现出色，可有效支持战术管制员的冲突解决决策。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [172] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RareDxR1，一个面向开放域罕见病诊断的端到端推理为中心的大语言模型，通过知识内化与自主进化学习框架，无需结构化表型和RAG，在多个基准上达到SOTA准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病鉴别诊断是一项关键但艰巨的临床任务，现有AI方法（基于管道的表型提取或检索增强生成）因预定义本体、检索瓶颈和缺乏诊断逻辑而导致关键信息丢失。

Method: 设计了渐进式端到端训练框架，结合知识内化与自主进化学习；提出反思增强推理采样（RERS）策略，通过从失败中学习合成专家级诊断轨迹；提出双层课程强化学习方法用于渐进掌握罕见病诊断。

Result: RareDxR1在不同基准测试上均达到最先进（SOTA）的准确率，标志着开放域罕见病诊断的重大突破。

Conclusion: 端到端的推理中心型LLM方法能够绕过结构化表型和封闭集决策的依赖，通过知识参数内化和自主进化学习显著提升罕见病诊断性能。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [173] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究双向私人信息下的人类-AI协作监督问题：人类私知自身奖励函数，AI私知所提动作的质量。基于CIRL和Oversight Game，引入具有双面非对称信息的情境赌博团队博弈，给出了单轮团队最优和近视行为规则的精确刻画，发现了“可避免伤害区域”——AI明知动作有害但近视人类因信任先验而拒绝监督。证明了该差距源于不可信的监督沟通，并分析了多轮动态中通过被动学习和主动信号如何部分缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI代理（如自主机器人）能够直接检视其人类监督者无法评估的情境时，会产生双向信息不对称：人类不清楚AI提议动作的实际质量，AI不清楚人类的真实偏好。这种信息结构使得人类监督的有效性受到根本性限制，现有工作缺乏对这种双向私人信息下监督博弈的精确刻画。

Method: 将问题建模为具有双面非对称信息的情境赌博团队博弈，引入play/ask/trust/oversee交互界面。通过去除物理状态转移（相对于完整POMDP），获得单轮精确刻画。分别求解团队最优解和近视行为规则，并分析二者的差距。进一步通过多轮动态分析（被动学习与主动信号）考察差距的演化。

Result: 得到了单轮团队最优和近视行为规则的精确刻画，发现二者之间存在“可避免伤害区域”——AI私知提议动作有害且应被关闭，但近视人类因信任先验而拒绝监督。证明了该差距本质上是不可信的监督沟通带来的代价。多轮动态中，该差距可通过被动学习和主动信号（使用一期滞后的监督响应）得到部分缓解，但未能完全消除。

Conclusion: 双向私人信息下的AI监督存在根本性的信息沟通瓶颈，近视人类的行为偏差会导致可预测且可避免的伤害。单轮视角下的差距揭示了人类-AI协作中监督不可信性的代价，而多轮动态虽能通过学习和信号部分改善，但无法完全弥合这一间隙。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [174] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL)概念框架，将AI素养重新定义为认知现象。通过对人机协作编程对话数据集的分析，发现78.8%的学生与GenAI互动缺乏认知投入，仅11.1%的互动表现出高认知参与水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知思维在学生在使用生成式AI学习过程中扮演核心角色，特别是在编程情境中，学习者需要构建查询、评估和验证AI生成输出，并调节问题解决策略。现有AI素养研究缺乏从认知过程角度理解人机交互的框架。

Method: 基于AIR认知框架（认知目标、理想和可靠认知过程），使用大规模人机协作编程对话数据集，识别可观察的认知目标维度（掌握导向目标）和认知过程维度（外包、寻求解释、寻求验证、提示监控和认知论证）。

Result: 78.8%的学生与GenAI互动依赖于非掌握导向目标和可靠性较低的认知策略（如外包和寻求验证）；仅有11.1%的互动表现出高认知参与，其中掌握导向目标与高级认知策略（如认知论证）相结合。

Conclusion: 学生在使用生成式AI进行编程学习时普遍缺乏认知性AI素养，大多数交互停留在低认知投入水平，需要教育干预来促进学生更可靠、更深入的认知过程。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [175] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在Lewis信号博弈中，智能体从零开始发明共享语言时，记忆架构比信道容量对协调效果影响更大。带持久笔记本的智能体表现最好（0.867±0.023），而纯上下文窗口的智能体在高容量时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在信号博弈中，两个智能体如何仅通过交互历史从零开始发明共享语言，并分析记忆架构与信道容量对协调效果的影响。

Method: 使用LLM智能体在Lewis信号博弈中，设置五种不同的记忆架构，并在不同信道容量配置下进行实验，比较各架构的协调性能。

Result: 带持久私人笔记本的智能体协调可靠性最高（0.867±0.023）；无状态智能体在中容量时达到峰值，高容量时因词汇增长超出上下文窗口跟踪能力而性能下降；信道容量等于物体数（容量=8）时反而是脆弱点，超额容量通常更好。

Conclusion: 信道容量不能单独预测协调效果，记忆架构决定了智能体能否将交互历史转化为稳定惯例，两者结合才能理解信号如何演变为语言。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [176] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0模型系列通过识别用户真实需求、构建可靠的评估体系，针对性解决长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大挑战，在推理、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平，初步具备处理复杂现实任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别用户的真实需求，构建可靠的、前瞻性的评估体系，解决复杂现实任务中的长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大持续挑战。

Method: 从用户真实需求出发，选择和抽象化相关基准来构建评估系统；针对长尾知识和复杂指令跟随进行改进；集成推理、视觉理解和搜索能力。

Result: Seed2.0在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平，初步展现出处理复杂现实任务的能力。

Conclusion: Seed2.0模型系列在解决复杂现实任务方面取得了有意义的进展，能够为数亿用户提供更大价值。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [177] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic事务处理（ATP）模型及其实践系统Mnemosyne，将生成式智能体产生的动作视为不可信提案，需通过声明式约束集C的确定性准入才能提交，从而在运行时保证状态正确性，独立于生成层的能力或诚实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM、求解器和智能体团队生成的工作流动作、修复和计划可能存在语法有效但过时、不可行、冲突或破坏触发修复证据的问题，现有方法缺乏对生成动作的事务性保障。

Method: 提出ATP事务模型，将生成动作视为不可信提案，通过声明式约束集C进行确定性准入；在Mnemosyne中实现追加式转换日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和活跃提交记录，并设计了局部化补偿修复协议（LCRP）。

Result: 在9项验证测试中成功拒绝目标违规行为同时接受有效工作，投影与验证开销低于6%，局部修复比全局重算少一个数量级的编辑操作。

Conclusion: ATP框架证明相对于约束集C的四个安全属性（权限分离、串行等价生成准入、证据保留修复、义务约束），以及LCRP的有界反应式修复保证，表明运行时提交模型可有效隔离生成层的不确定性。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [178] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\system{}的离散时间控制系统，通过五种执行齿轮（观察、建议、规划、执行、交互）与效用门控调度和事件驱动回退相结合，解决了自主智能体在无持续人类监督下的安全违规、行为不稳定和连续性丢失问题。单智能体情况下证明了单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性、回退完备性等特性；多智能体CPS中结合\smart{}管理自主生命周期，通过共识门控、群体Lyapunov分析等提供分布式安全保证。在基于NIST数据集的三智能体UR5机器人装配单元实验中，实现了99.6%的异常检测率（对比基线2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供了形式化物理工作空间安全证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体（包括LLM驱动软件智能体和机器人物理智能体）在无持续人类监督运行时面临三类常见故障模式：未验证动作导致的安全违规、无约束循环导致的行为不稳定、未处理错误状态导致的连续性丢失。现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架来同时保证安全性和稳定性。

Method: 提出\system{}离散时间控制系统，包含五种执行齿轮（Gobs、Gsug、Gplan、Gexec、Gint）作为微级权限，结合效用门控调度和事件驱动回退机制。单智能体场景建立齿轮约束马尔可夫决策过程等效性；多智能体CPS场景应用\smart{}管理自主生命周期，将运行时证据映射到四个治理状态（稳定/元/辅助/监管），通过共识门控、群体Lyapunov分析、每智能体齿轮权限和会合控制提供分布式安全保证。

Result: 在三智能体UR5机器人装配单元上，基于NIST机器人手臂位置精度退化测量数据集进行10,000次蒙特卡洛实验，实现99.6%异常检测率（对比单智能体基线2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供形式化物理工作空间安全证书。

Conclusion: 执行齿轮作为\smart{}运行时治理状态下的微级权限，实现了动作控制与自主治理的分离，为自主智能体系统提供了统一的安全性和稳定性理论保证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>


### [179] [Personalization as Inverse Planning: Learning Latent Design Intents for Agentic Slide Generation via Structural Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00407)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,jing Gao,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra,Wei-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPIRE是一个通过强化学习训练双智能体协同去噪来间接实现页面级幻灯片个性化设计的框架，将幻灯片个性化设计（PSP）形式化为逆向规划问题，在无需预设模板或用户详细指令的情况下，通过结构化去噪作为PSP的一致替代目标来学习设计意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理方法依赖预设模板或用户冗长指令，无法捕获细粒度的页面级设计意图，导致页面级幻灯片个性化设计（PSP）问题尚未解决。

Method: 将PSP形式化为逆向规划问题，通过故意破坏干净幻灯片的视觉结构，创建一个可验证的结构化去噪任务；利用两个智能体通过强化学习协同优化可执行设计；理论证明结构化去噪是PSP的一致替代目标，且多智能体公式可严格降低RL中的策略梯度方差。

Result: 大量实验表明SPIRE在幻灯片个性化设计任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPIRE通过结构化去噪作为代理任务，结合多智能体强化学习，有效解决了无需预设模板和用户详细指令情况下的页面级幻灯片个性化设计问题。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>


### [180] [PHREEQC-MCQ-200: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Scientific Simulator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00436)
*Ke Zhang,Sahchit Chundur,Mohammad Javad Qomi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准，用于评估工具增强型智能体在地球化学模拟中的表现。研究发现，模拟器访问显著提升整体准确率，但存在非单调性增益——工具增强会导致部分原本正确回答的题目出错。输出访问协议（如目录接口）对强模型有优化效果，但对中等模型反而降低性能。研究呼吁评估科学工具使用时需报告项目级保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败等细粒度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体与科学软件的连接日益增多，但工具访问何时使科学计算更可靠而非更复杂尚不明确。现有评估多聚焦于工具调用能力，缺乏对端到端科学计算可靠性的系统诊断。

Method: 构建PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准，包含200道选择题，源自21个经验证的PHREEQC水化学场景。评估多系列前沿及中等模型在有无模拟器访问条件下的准确率，并分析输出访问协议（如目录接口）对性能的影响。

Result: 模拟器访问显著提升整体准确率，但增益非单调——工具增强导致部分原本正确回答的题目出错。目录接口可降低强模型token成本并保持/提升准确率，但会降低无法可靠导航结构化输出的中等模型性能。

Conclusion: PHREEQC-MCQ-200将科学工具使用定位为端到端诊断问题而非简单工具调用能力。评估科学智能体时需报告项目级保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败及计算链断点等细粒度指标。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>


### [181] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE 是一个闭环框架，通过检索增强的多智能体 LLM 推理与 APSIM 生物物理模拟相结合，生成并验证农艺建议，解决了静态指南与 LLM 建议的局限性。实验表明，Tree of Thoughts 实现最高产量，而 Reflexion 以更低计算成本达到相近效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的农业咨询系统面临双重困境：静态农艺指南无法应对季节性变化和动态不确定性，而基于 LLM 的建议又存在农艺上可信但生理学上不可靠的风险。因此需要一种既能生成建议又能验证其科学合理性的闭环框架。

Method: 提出 Agri-SAGE 框架，集成检索增强的多智能体 LLM 推理与 APSIM 生物物理模拟，实现"生成-验证"闭环。对比三种推理策略：Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts 和 Reflexion，在 10 年回顾性分析中评估。

Result: 三种推理方法均显著优于静态 Package-of-Practice 基线。Tree of Thoughts 达到最高产量峰值，而 Reflexion 借助跨季节情景记忆，在相当农艺效果下大幅降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 检索增强的多智能体 LLM 结合生物物理模拟能有效提升农艺建议质量，且不同的推理策略可在性能与计算成本之间灵活权衡。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [182] [Multi-scale Mixture of World Models for Embodied Agents in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00457)
*Jinwoo Jang,Daniel J. Rho,Sihyung Yoon,Hyunsuk Cho,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MuSix 提出了一种基于尺度的 Mixture of Experts 框架，通过两阶段路由和尺度依赖的遗忘机制，实现了具身智能体在多尺度推理和动态适应上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Mixture of Experts (MoE) 在具身智能体应用中面临两个挑战：(1) 路由缺乏显式的尺度概念，无法在特定尺度上进行针对性更新；(2) 统一更新策略无法适应不同尺度知识以不同速率过时的情况。

Method: 提出 MuSix 框架，包含：(1) 两阶段路由机制：元路由器基于体验距离（受解释水平理论启发）将情境新颖性映射到连续尺度空间的权重，随后每个尺度的基路由器在识别出的尺度内选择世界模型；(2) 尺度依赖的遗忘机制：低尺度知识快速更新，高尺度抽象持续保留，并通过门控跨尺度转移维持层级间的一致性。

Result: 在 EmbodiedBench 和 HAZARD 基准上，MuSix 在多尺度推理和动态适应任务中优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模尺度感知的路由和适应机制，MuSix 有效解决了具身智能体中多尺度知识更新与迁移的难题，为动态现实环境中的智能体部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>


### [183] [AI Native Games: A Survey and Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00527)
*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI原生游戏的定义标准（反事实判据：若移除AI组件则核心玩法崩溃或根本改变），基于此筛选出53款游戏/原型，提出G/N双轴分类法（G轴：玩家面对的游戏类型；N轴：使生成式AI不可或缺的AI机制），发现当前作品集中在语言前向设计（叙事冒险、认知交互、生成叙事），而语义裁决、多智能体模拟、生成建造、关系/同伴玩法等类别较少。核心设计问题是组织语义开放性为稳定玩法，依赖机械不变量（目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性），最后提出可控生成、AI即机制设计、多模态多智能体、推理经济学、评估、安全与监管路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已能运行时生成对话、任务、角色、图像和世界，但仅凭生成能力并不构成AI原生游戏，也不保证可玩性。现有对"AI原生"的定义模糊不清，缺乏可操作的区分标准，导致无法系统分析这类游戏的设计空间和发展方向。

Method: 提出反事实判据定义AI原生游戏（核心循环中运行时生成式AI具有构成性地位），据此筛选候选作品并分析53个公开可用的AI原生游戏与原型；构建G/N双轴分类法（G轴=游戏类型，N轴=AI机制），对语料库进行系统分类与分析。

Result: 语料库集中在语言前向设计（叙事冒险、认知交互、生成叙事），语义裁决、多智能体模拟、生成建造、关系/同伴玩法等类别代表性不足。核心设计挑战在于将语义开放性组织为稳定游戏玩法，需依赖机械不变量（目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性）。

Conclusion: AI原生游戏设计的核心问题是可控生成、AI即机制设计、多模态与多智能体系统、推理经济学、评估方法、安全与监管，据此提出未来发展路线图。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>


### [184] [HARC: Coupling Harmfulness and Refusal Directions for Robust Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00572)
*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对齐LLM中安全表征的机制：模型在提示端和响应端存在独立的harmfulness和refusal方向，越狱通过抑制这些方向生效。基于此提出HARC微调方法，通过在harmfulness-refusal子空间内耦合两个方向，在不损害通用能力的前提下显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对齐LLM内部如何表征安全对于诊断对齐漏洞至关重要，因为它能解释越狱为何成功，并为设计稳健的对齐策略提供依据。

Method: 1) 分析表明模型在提示端token位置将harmfulness和refusal编码为残差流中可分离的方向，越狱通过抑制这些方向生效；2) 发现响应端模型在生成有害内容时能识别其有害性，即使提示端未能识别；3) 提出HARC微调方法，在harmfulness-refusal子空间内耦合提示端和响应端的两个方向。

Result: HARC在六种基线方法（涵盖训练时和推理时安全方法）中实现了最强的鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡，且不降低通用能力或增加过度拒绝。harmfulness和refusal方向在五个模型家族和两种规模上跨模型迁移，无需架构特定调整。

Conclusion: 越狱攻击通过在提示端抑制harmfulness或refusal方向实现，而模型在响应端仍能识别有害内容。HARC通过耦合两个端点的安全方向，在不牺牲通用能力的前提下显著提升了安全性，且具有良好的跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Understanding how aligned LLMs internally represent safety is critical for diagnosing alignment vulnerabilities, as it explains why jailbreaks succeed and informs the design of robust alignment strategies. Prior work shows that aligned LLMs encode harmfulness and refusal as separable directions in the residual stream at prompt-side token positions. We show that jailbreaks succeed at prompt encoding by suppressing either the refusal or harmfulness direction before any token is generated, with distinct attack classes occupying separable regions of the harmfulness-refusal plane. Extending the analysis to response-token positions, we find that the model recognizes harmful content while it is generating that content, even when it failed to recognize the input as harmful at the prompt side. Motivated by our findings, we introduce HARC (Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling), a fine-tuning method that pairs the two directions across both prompt and response positions. Since the intervention is confined to the harmfulness-refusal subspace, it leaves the rest of the residual stream intact and does not degrade general capability or inflate over-refusal. Across extensive experiments, HARC achieves the strongest robustness-capability-usability trade-off among six baselines spanning the major training-time and inference-time safety methods. The harmfulness and refusal directions at prompt and response positions transfer across the five model families and two scales we tested without architecture-specific tuning.

</details>


### [185] [AGI Maze as a Benchmark Framework for World-Modeling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00627)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGI Maze框架，用于构建需要智能体学习并使用世界状态表征的网格迷宫任务。实验表明，普通LLM在推理时无法内部表征迷宫，即使允许使用消息历史作为工作记忆，LLM智能体仍无法在充足步数内可靠解决小型迷宫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在默认的下一个token预测模式下无法可靠地产生持久的、可操作的外部世界表征。许多看似"推理"的任务在部分可观测、有状态且需要记忆和结构化假设的环境中变得困难，因此需要构建能测试智能体世界状态表征能力的基准。

Method: 设计AGI Maze框架，提供一系列基于网格的迷宫任务，具有简洁API和多个难度级别。框架不依赖高维感知输入，创建需要智能体学习并使用世界状态表征的基准。评估了多种普通LLM在简单迷宫上的表现，并引入基线智能体，允许其使用消息历史作为工作记忆来构建观察描述。

Result: 普通LLM在推理时无法内部表征迷宫。即使允许使用消息历史作为工作记忆，LLM智能体在步数预算远超人类所需的情况下，仍无法可靠解决小型迷宫。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在需要持久世界状态表征和结构化假设的任务中表现不足，AGI Maze框架为评估和推动这方面的研究提供了有效基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful pattern-completion systems, but their default operating mode - predicting the next token from a static context - does not reliably produce persistent, manipulable representations of an external world. Many tasks that look like "reasoning" in text become substantially harder once the environment is partially observable, stateful, and requires memory and structured hypotheses about hidden state. AGI Maze is a lightweight framework for building such environments without requiring high-dimensional sensory inputs. It provides a family of grid-based maze tasks with a clean API and multiple difficulty regimes. The goal is to create benchmarks where agents must learn and use world state representations, not just infer a local rule over readily provided observations. We provide an initial evaluation of several vanilla LLMs on simple mazes showing that they fail to represent mazes internally at LLM inference time. We also introduce a baseline agent, which is allowed to use its message history as a working memory to construct descriptions of observations at agentic runtime. Although this can improve performance, it is still insufficient for an LLM agent to reliably solve even small mazes within a step budget that is more than enough for humans.

</details>


### [186] [Coachable agents for interactive gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00642)
*Roberto Capobianco,Harm van Seijen,Nolan D. Bard,Neil Burch,Fatima Davelouis,Josh Davidson,Alisa Devlic,Yunshu Du,Ishan Durugkar,Siddhant Gangapurwala,Daniel Hernandez,G. Zacharias Holland,Sahil Jain,Kenta Kawamoto,Raksha Kumaraswamy,Patrick MacAlpine,Dustin R. Morrill,Declan Oller,Francesco Riccio,Akanksha Saran,Craig Sherstan,Kaushik Subramanian,Thomas J. Walsh,Samuel Barrett,Kizza N. Frisbee,Mady Govil,Johannes Günther,Varun R. Kompella,James A. MacGlashan,Maxwell Svetlik,Michael D. Thomure,Jaden B. Travnik,Kevin Waugh,Elahe Aghapour,Florian Fuchs,Andreanne Lemay,Shruti Mishra,Takuma Seno,Peter Stone,Michael Spranger,Peter R. Wurman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合通用价值函数近似器（UVFA）、精心选择的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强的框架，使智能体在复杂领域（如《地平线：西之绝境》、《GT赛车》和类人机器人行走）中能够展现多样化的"风格"行为。用户可在运行时灵活选择最终行为，在满足核心任务的同时保持对行为风格的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常只学习一种接近最优的行为来解决任务，但许多实际应用场景需要用户能够实时控制任务被执行的方式（即"风格"），现有方法缺乏这种灵活的行为调控能力。

Method: 将通用价值函数近似器（UVFA）与精心设计的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强相结合，构建一个能够指导智能体展现多样化风格的训练框架。

Result: 在《地平线：西之绝境》、《GT赛车》和开源类人机器人测试域中，智能体均能在满足核心任务的同时，对风格指令表现出强一致性。不同领域（赛车、游戏战斗、类人行走）均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架允许终端用户在运行时灵活选择最终行为，实现了对智能体执行风格的可控性，拓展了强化学习在需要行为多样性场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven to be a valuable tool in the creation of advanced AI and robotic systems, contributing to everything from game playing to robotics to foundation models. Through trial-and-error, these AI systems typically learn one, near-optimal behavior to solve their tasks. However, there are many use cases in which one would like to assert some level of control, preferably in real time, over how the task is solved. We refer to these modifications of a core task as styles. We combine universal value function approximators (UVFAs) with carefully selected training scenarios, learning algorithms, and data augmentation to create a framework for coaching agents that exhibit styles in complex domains. We demonstrate the framework's application in the AAA video games Horizon Forbidden West and Gran Turismo, and in an open-source humanoid test domain. Despite the different nature of the domains -- car racing, stylized game combat, and humanoid walking -- each agent shows strong coherence to the style requests while still satisfying the main task in its domain. Importantly, the techniques outlined in this paper allow an end user to choose the final behavior at run time, giving them flexible control over the final executed performance.

</details>


### [187] [Self-GC: Self-Governing Context for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00692)
*Xubin Hao,Hongjin Meng,Xin Yin,Jiawei Zhu,Chenpeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-GC 提出了一种将 LLM 智能体上下文视为可索引、可恢复对象的生命周期管理框架，通过侧通道规划器执行折叠、掩码和剪枝操作，在长程任务中实现了比传统启发式方法更高的无影响剪枝率（Hard Set 上 84.85% vs 54.55%-69.70%），并在生产中降低 10%-15% 的日间平均输入 Token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体在长程交互中累积的大量结构化上下文（工具结果、文件、计划、用户约束等）无法被简单当作可丢弃的文本来处理。当前方法要么依赖运行时启发式（如时序剪枝、工具输出掩码），要么在上下文限制附近做最终自摘要，但启发式缺乏对未来依赖的预见性，摘要虽保留叙事状态却隐藏了精确证据、定位信息和可编辑工件。

Method: 将用户轮次、工具跨度、技能状态转化为可索引对象；引入侧通道规划器（side-channel planner）来建议折叠（fold）、掩码（mask）和剪枝（prune）操作；通过框架强制执行可恢复侧车（recoverable sidecars）、安全提交边界（safe commit boundaries）和缓存感知提交（cache-aware commit）。

Result: 在 33 会话的 Hard Set 上，剪枝 43.95% 的前缀 Token，84.85% 的未来延续不受影响（基线为 54.55%-69.70%）；在 332 会话的生产套件上，三种规划器骨干达到 91.27%-94.58% 的无影响率（基线为 77.71%-87.46%）；在线账户级拆分使日间平均输入 Token 减少 10%-15%，峰值减少近 20%。

Conclusion: 上下文管理应被视为对可索引、可恢复对象的运行时生命周期控制，而非事后文本清理。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate tool results, files, plans, and user constraints that are too structured to be treated as a disposable text suffix. Current systems mostly rely on in-run heuristics such as chronological pruning and tool-output masking, or on final self-summary near a context limit. Heuristics are cheap but blind to future dependencies; summaries preserve narrative state but often hide exact evidence, locators, and editable artifacts. We present Self-GC, where GC denotes self-governing context while deliberately echoing garbage collection: the system does not merely reclaim unused tokens, but governs the lifecycle of agent context objects. Self-GC turns user turns, tool spans, and skill state into indexed objects; asks a side-channel planner to propose fold, mask, and prune actions; and lets the harness enforce recoverable sidecars, safe commit boundaries, and cache-aware commit. On a 33-session Hard Set, Self-GC prunes 43.95% of prefix tokens while leaving 84.85% of future continuations unaffected, compared with no-impact rates of 54.55% to 69.70% for heuristic baselines. On a 332-session production-derived suite, three planner backbones reach no-impact rates of 91.27% to 94.58%, while baselines remain at 77.71% to 87.46%. In production, an online account-level split reduces daytime average input tokens by 10% to 15%, with peak reductions near 20%. These results point to context management as runtime lifecycle control over indexed, recoverable objects rather than post hoc text cleanup.

</details>


### [188] [Self-Evolving Agents with Anytime-Valid Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00871)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SEA架构通过将自我进化限制在小型适配器和版本化防护栏内，利用随时有效的验证门控机制（基于固定错误预算）和五种验证器机制，在不改变冻结基础模型的前提下实现可控的自我修改。在SWE-bench测试中，基础能力是主要影响因素，而SEA套件在强基模型上贡献了+4到+5的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化代理违反了大多数学习理论保证的假设——数据、评估器、组件和假设空间都是由正在更新的策略产生的，这导致缺乏理论保证和安全性保障。

Method: 提出SEA架构：(1) 将自我修改限制在小型转向适配器和版本化防护栏内；(2) 使用随时有效的门控机制（anytime-valid gate），基于固定错误预算发出可审计证书；(3) 五种环路控制器组合已有保证；(4) 五种验证器机制（best-of-N、微步搜索、自创再现预言机、搜索层控制、自我修复）提供密集的无评分器信号；(5) 仅从问题文本计算信号。

Result: 在52实例SWE-bench验证子集上，使用四个基础模型：基础能力是主导的、无混杂因素的效应；在强基模型上，无操作复合控制隔离出套件贡献为+4和+5（GLM 5.2: 24→28；GPT: 29→34，即65%最佳）。事件日志确认机制触发并防止了回归。

Conclusion: SEA架构能够在不违反学习理论保证的前提下实现可控的自我进化，通过随时有效的验证机制确保安全性。当前结果为单次运行，确认运行间方差和适配每任务算法组合是未来工作。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents violate the assumption behind most learning-theoretic guarantees: the data, evaluator, components, and hypothesis space are produced by the policy being updated. We present \textbf{SEA}, an architecture that confines self-modification to a small steering adapter and a versioned harness around a \emph{frozen} base model and admits each modification only through an anytime-valid gate that emits an auditable certificate against a fixed error budget. Five loop controllers compose published guarantees; because such gates can only \emph{select} among behaviors the frozen base already produces, five verifier-in-the-loop mechanisms -- best-of-$N$, micro-step search, self-authored reproduction oracles, search-layer control, and self-repair -- supply the dense, grader-free signal the gates require, computed from the issue text alone.
